DM: Yeah.

%% u4016:0-64 %%
Kenzo: ... you know, chapter moment before we start in with the next...

%% u4017:0-19 %%
DM: That's a good idea.

%% u4018:0-10, u4019:0-47, u4020:0-4, u4021:0-29 %%
Kenzo: I like it. Feeling a little like I want to get outside or- Yeah ... not the end of the world.

%% u4022:0-67 %%
DM: This would be, we could do lunch and beach and then come back here.

%% u4023:0-36 %%
Kenzo: I, I think that's, that sounds good.

%% u4024:0-30 %%
Seeker: This has been an epic session-

%% u4025:0-4 %%
DM: Yeah.

%% u4026:0-18 %%
Seeker: ... already today.

%% u4027:0-10 %%
Kenzo: Holy fuck.

%% u4028:0-42 %%
DM: [laughs] That is a good, good break point.

%% u4029:0-28 %%
Seeker: Uh, do you want me to check?

%% u4030:0-9 %%
Kenzo: Oh, yeah.

%% u4031:0-19 %%
Wellby: Oh, I love the crab.

%% u4032:0-55, u4033:0-5, u4034:0-32, u4035:0-31, u4036:0-14, u4037:0-15, u4038:0-4 %%
DM: [gentle music] I'm just gonna get a little bit of this. Okay. I'll put that there. [coughs]

So in the village as the sun sets, um, there is a feast prepared. The-

%% u4039:0-8 %%
Seeker: That was.

%% u4040:0-3, u4041:0-39, u4042:0-3, u4043:0-87, u4044:0-120, u4045:0-42, u4046:0-95, u4047:0-3, u4048:0-20, u4049:0-19 %%
DM: ... the Te'kula almost never have visitors, and so many at once is an occasion for celebration, even despite the, the sort of thread of uncertainty and, and doubt that, that hangs over this revelation from the two pieces of jade meeting, and, um, Alayah's, uh, uncertainty about what this all means. During dinner, dinner is the four of you, Alayah, the chief Yota, three or four other Deno'qai, um, and the elf Belegor. Theba is not there.

%% u4050:0-8 %%
Seeker: [coughs]

%% u4051:0-76, u4052:0-10, u4053:0-13, u4054:0-66, u4055:0-34, u4056:0-20, u4057:0-7, u4058:0-72, u4059:0-50, u4060:0-19, u4061:0-3, u4062:0-128, u4063:0-53, u4064:0-87, u4065:0-123, u4066:0-22 %%
DM: She has said she's, after learning that Mezzar was a dragon, she has to pray and think. And dinner is a somewhat boisterous affair without much chance for conversation.

But as the evening winds down, um, uh, Belegor the elf, Alayah, um, and a few others, um, come sit around the fire on the village green. Belegor particularly keen to hear Delwath's story, and Alayah kind of, uh, having spent so much of her childhood and her young life ensconced in these dreams, and now finally having met somebody else who has also experienced these, especially in the past... You get the sense that the past five years since Jordo died have not been easy for her.

So she is sort of following Kenzo around a little bit trying, you know, constantly asking him about, "Do you remember this? Do you remember that?"

%% u4067:0-5 %%
Seeker: Yeah.

%% u4068:0-70 %%
DM: Finally having somebody to talk about what these things actually mean-

%% u4069:0-8 %%
Seeker: Mm. Yeah.

%% u4070:0-3, u4071:0-33, u4072:0-104, u4073:0-90, u4074:0-107, u4075:0-85, u4076:0-3, u4077:0-48, u4078:0-7, u4079:0-23, u4080:0-11, u4081:0-30 %%
DM: ... has clearly had an impact on her. But everyb- as everybody settles down, um, Belegor, who is a, a tall elf, um, over six feet, 6'2", 6'3", like all of them ageless, um, with copper hair, short, relatively short cropped, um, wavy, uh, pale skin, green eyes, wearing sort of brown, brown, clothes in browns and reds, very autumnal colored, um, with a high collar and sleeves bound a- around his wrists by, like, gold threads.

Um, he, he turns to Delwath and he says, "I, I think perhaps we knew each other once in passing. You are of the 36th Kah, yes?"

%% u4082:0-4 %%
Delwath: Yes.

%% u4083:0-14 %%
Seeker: The 36th what?

%% u4084:0-4 %%
Delwath: Kah.

%% u4085:0-79 %%
DM: Generation. The 36th gener- 36th generation of elves ever to walk on the earth.

%% u4086:0-63 %%
Seeker: Would Seeker already know about this kind of terminology or no?

%% u4087:0-42 %%
Delwath: You probably haven't had that word before.

%% u4088:0-34, u4089:0-8 %%
Seeker: So that was in character, I guess. [laughs]

%% u4090:0-90 %%
DM: Yeah, and that's what Belegor says, is the 36th generation of elves to walk on this earth.

%% u4091:0-18 %%
Delwath: What has happened?

%% u4092:0-31, u4093:0-28 %%
DM: I, I would ask the same of you. How did you come to be here?

%% u4094:0-21, u4095:0-28, u4096:0-54 %%
Delwath: That's quite a story, which I'm happy to tell, but first tell me what has happened in the last 200 years.

%% u4097:0-8 %%
Seeker: [laughs]

%% u4098:0-8 %%
DM: [laughs]

%% u4099:0-33 %%
Seeker: That's a ridiculous thing to say.

%% u4100:0-21 %%
Kenzo: Pulls out a cold one.

%% u4101:0-31, u4102:0-24 %%
DM: You, you know, you know not the tragedies of our people?

%% u4103:0-28 %%
Delwath: No, not since the Great War.

%% u4104:0-26, u4105:0-19 %%
DM: Like, you must know of the wars that followed.

%% u4106:0-5 %%
Delwath: Some.

%% u4107:0-16, u4108:0-3, u4109:0-11, u4110:0-4, u4111:0-86, u4112:0-15 %%
DM: The dragon fire, the hobgoblins, the, the fight against the creeping darkness of the monsters of the Shadowfell spawned when Cha'mutte died.

%% u4113:0-21, u4114:0-15, u4115:0-51 %%
Delwath: Only what I've heard. Been in Dunmar. We don't talk of what happened up north much there.

%% u4116:0-3 %%
DM: Mm.

%% u4117:0-30, u4118:0-63 %%
Delwath: Is there still a... I don't... What is the... Did you make up a name for Delwath's home place?

%% u4119:0-3 %%
DM: Um-

%% u4120:0-35 %%
Delwath: I don't think I ever knew it though.

%% u4121:0-25, u4122:0-50, u4123:0-25 %%
DM: ... well, do you want to, do you want your home to be a city that's vanished or that's been destroyed?

%% u4124:0-8 %%
Unknown: [laughs]

%% u4125:0-23 %%
Seeker: Those are your choices.

%% u4126:0-22 %%
Wellby: Vanished or destroyed.

%% u4127:0-8 %%
Unknown: [laughs]

%% u4128:0-38 %%
Delwath: Ah, destroyed. I'll go with destroyed.

%% u4129:0-5, u4130:0-14, u4131:0-10 %%
DM: Okay. How about, um, Volandeer?

%% u4132:0-10, u4133:0-63 %%
Delwath: Volandeer. W- What has hap- I mean, that's where he's from too, obviously.

%% u4134:0-5 %%
DM: Yeah.

%% u4135:0-3, u4136:0-31 %%
Delwath: Um, what has happened to Volandeer?

%% u4137:0-6, u4138:0-15, u4139:0-36, u4140:0-8, u4141:0-8, u4142:0-13 %%
DM: Burned by dragon fire. Like many of the other great cities, Terrasa, Elintor, all destroyed.

%% u4143:0-19, u4144:0-10 %%
Delwath: There are any left? I've seen-

%% u4145:0-11 %%
Seeker: Any cities?

%% u4146:0-8, u4147:0-19, u4148:0-30 %%
Delwath: No elves save you since, uh, since returning to this place.

%% u4149:0-18, u4150:0-34, u4151:0-14, u4152:0-39, u4153:0-60, u4154:0-15, u4155:0-16, u4156:0-22 %%
DM: Perhaps somewhere. Many of us came back from the wars scarred, hurt. [coughs] We mourn the destroyed cities. This is return from war to society changed, many lives lost, and many cities simply vanished. To where, we knew not.

%% u4157:0-33 %%
Delwath: We've not been able to find them?

%% u4158:0-63 %%
DM: Some think they fled to other planes, whole cities transported.

%% u4159:0-13 %%
Seeker: Whole cities?

%% u4160:0-29 %%
DM: We have searched the Feywild.

%% u4161:0-12 %%
Seeker: Transported?

%% u4162:0-15, u4163:0-46, u4164:0-3, u4165:0-10, u4166:0-74, u4167:0-33, u4168:0-3, u4169:0-21, u4170:0-49, u4171:0-34, u4172:0-67, u4173:0-5, u4174:0-6, u4175:0-11 %%
DM: Others think... Some of my generation, some of our generation, uh, think that many elves went to fight evil and protect this world across the multiverse and brought fortresses with them.

But personally, I believe that the unraveling that was started by Cha'mutte and never finished because he died still claimed those closest to his home, the furthest north cities. Holds where, I know not.

%% u4176:0-11, u4177:0-25, u4178:0-35 %%
Seeker: Did you say defending the multiverse? Wha- what do you mean by that word?

%% u4179:0-44, u4180:0-36, u4181:0-68 %%
DM: There are many planes not this, the Feywild, the Shadowfell, places like Arborea, the realms of the gods, the planes of fire and earth, air and water.

%% u4182:0-51, u4183:0-21, u4184:0-44 %%
Seeker: I'm fascinated by and a student of these phenomena, but still an amateur, and you use that word to encompass them all?

%% u4185:0-7 %%
DM: Indeed.

%% u4186:0-33 %%
Seeker: What does it mean to defend them?

%% u4187:0-94 %%
DM: Well, the battles that were fought here are not necessarily the only battles of the Great War.

%% u4188:0-4 %%
Seeker: Huh.

%% u4189:0-11, u4190:0-73, u4191:0-53, u4192:0-20 %%
DM: I know not. But I believe if whole cities vanished, they vanished against their will, and the bulk of the elves may still be lost somewhere in some other plane.

%% u4193:0-35 %%
Kenzo: How, how would a whole city vanish?

%% u4194:0-15 %%
Seeker: Powerful magic.

%% u4195:0-17, u4196:0-30, u4197:0-56, u4198:0-22, u4199:0-19, u4200:0-16, u4201:0-31, u4202:0-16 %%
DM: The process of... The structure of this world is made of strands of many other planes captured and tamed, or so the elves teach. Cha'mutte sought to pull those apart to bring the other planes fully within this one.

%% u4203:0-4 %%
Seeker: Hmm.

%% u4204:0-59, u4205:0-18 %%
DM: To collapse the boundaries that had kept reality stable for several lifetimes.

%% u4206:0-39 %%
Seeker: When an elf says lifetime, they mean...

%% u4207:0-15 %%
Kenzo: It's millennia.

%% u4208:0-10, u4209:0-9, u4210:0-54, u4211:0-57, u4212:0-56, u4213:0-35, u4214:0-46, u4215:0-25, u4216:0-13, u4217:0-16, u4218:0-28, u4219:0-30, u4220:0-13 %%
DM: Millennia, at least. If that process started and then was abruptly aborted, perhaps some parts of this world snapped to other planes.

And I still hope perhaps that a city of the elves, maybe Oromondi, one of our furthest north settlements, beautiful place in the mountains, city of pines and spruce, snow and ice, quartz, crystal, perhaps it survives somehow, protected by our people on the plane of air.

%% u4221:0-4 %%
Kenzo: Hmm.

%% u4222:0-46 %%
DM: Perhaps there might be a way to bring it back.

%% u4223:0-50 %%
Seeker: How does, how does one travel to the plane of air?

%% u4224:0-41 %%
Delwath: Do you know if there are other survivors?

%% u4225:0-15, u4226:0-51 %%
DM: There are some. The thirty-seventh generation is not lost entirely.

%% u4227:0-15, u4228:0-27, u4229:0-16, u4230:0-3, u4231:0-2, u4232:0-6 %%
Delwath: Do you know if, uh, do you know of Rathion? Do you know him? Or, or Drago?

%% u4233:0-34 %%
DM: Am I supposed to know these names?

%% u4234:0-42 %%
Delwath: I'm just making names up. They would be...

%% u4235:0-51, u4236:0-5 %%
DM: They're Delwath's friend, like, friends of Delwath. Okay.

%% u4237:0-59, u4238:0-65, u4239:0-52, u4240:0-23 %%
Delwath: They're just random people I'm ma- ma- making up right now. You can decide how, how, how, how dark is Delwath's current life. Are his fri-- Is everybody he was close to all dead? He could not know them.

%% u4241:0-22 %%
DM: I do not know Rathion.

%% u4242:0-46, u4243:0-3, u4244:0-26 %%
Kenzo: Ah, yes. He was maimed in a horrible accident. Oh. Eaten to death by a snake.

%% u4245:0-10 %%
Unknown: [laughing]

%% u4246:0-34 %%
Wellby: Oh, and him maimed and then eaten.

%% u4247:0-10 %%
Unknown: [laughing]

%% u4248:0-66 %%
Kenzo: His corpse dragged, dragged through a swamp where he ran to death.

%% u4249:0-22, u4250:0-30, u4251:0-5 %%
DM: Laston, I, I know him. I fought with him in the wars. Pete-

%% u4252:0-28, u4253:0-6 %%
Wellby: [laughs] His horrible death. Sorry.

%% u4254:0-5 %%
DM: Pete-

%% u4255:0-19 %%
Wellby: Go ahead. No, sorry.

%% u4256:0-58, u4257:0-12, u4258:0-69, u4259:0-44, u4260:0-6, u4261:0-39, u4262:0-44, u4263:0-7 %%
DM: ... would be thematically appropriate for him to opt dead.

He returned. He was, he was one of those in our generation who turned inward after the wars. There are some hidden cities left, a few, mostly north of here, Forest of Dreams. Small, nothing like the grand cities of old. Hidden.

%% u4264:0-29 %%
Delwath: Do you know how to find them?

%% u4265:0-41, u4266:0-19, u4267:0-28, u4268:0-31, u4269:0-4, u4270:0-50, u4271:0-30, u4272:0-19 %%
DM: I'm sure they would not be hidden to you. I, I could not face a lifetime of turning inward after my children were born and left their body parents to join their generation mates. Left their what? Body parents? Their body parents.

%% u4273:0-3 %%
Delwath: Oh.

%% u4274:0-6, u4275:0-6, u4276:0-15, u4277:0-43, u4278:0-44, u4279:0-2, u4280:0-44 %%
DM: I left again. They are hidden in a small camp in the north, I, I suspect. I haven't spoken to them in quite some time. I, I needed to know what was left of the world.

%% u4281:0-23, u4282:0-36, u4283:0-23, u4284:0-65 %%
Delwath: To me, the war was only years ago, a small number, one, two. I was in the Shadowfell since the Great War until nine months ago when I met these folks.

%% u4285:0-26 %%
DM: How did that thing happen?

%% u4286:0-10, u4287:0-16, u4288:0-46, u4289:0-33, u4290:0-30, u4291:0-30, u4292:0-24, u4293:0-130, u4294:0-8, u4295:0-30, u4296:0-12 %%
Delwath: I suspect, uh... I was, um, I was the quartermaster of a company of elves. We encountered a hobgoblin party, and a great battle was fought. All of the elves died save me. All the hobgoblins died.

And as I was trying to pick through the bodies and uncover the body of my commander, she was lying underneath this great hobgoblin general. I touched something on his arm by accident.

%% u4297:0-7 %%
DM: Mm-hmm.

%% u4298:0-40, u4299:0-43, u4300:0-18, u4301:0-17, u4302:0-5, u4303:0-6, u4304:0-46, u4305:0-33, u4306:0-27, u4307:0-37, u4308:0-37, u4309:0-37, u4310:0-44 %%
Delwath: And it transported me to the Shadowfell, where I was trapped re- reliving the battle for what felt like several lifetimes until a god, Yezali as he was known, or as they were known, but now taking the name Aaviskar, somehow also trapped there.

I think, I think he was one of the... That he, they were one of the Tanshi. I'm sure they were one of the Tanshi, and somehow they found a way to send me out.

%% u4311:0-19 %%
DM: Did you say Yezali?

%% u4312:0-12 %%
Delwath: Yezali, yes.

%% u4313:0-19 %%
DM: Have you met Ariel?

%% u4314:0-11 %%
Delwath: I have not.

%% u4315:0-28 %%
DM: She has mentioned that name.

%% u4316:0-35 %%
Delwath: I would very much like to meet her.

%% u4317:0-16 %%
Kenzo: Yezali is, uh...

%% u4318:0-61, u4319:0-87 %%
Delwath: In visions, I saw him, I saw them die defending their people, but also touched the bane of a great, by a great demon, and appeared in the Shadowfell.

%% u4320:0-14, u4321:0-35, u4322:0-6, u4323:0-14, u4324:0-30, u4325:0-18, u4326:0-6, u4327:0-18, u4328:0-23 %%
DM: A great demon? I fought many demons after the war. I fear there was some portal to the abyss in Pandar, fueled Cha'mutte's reign, perhaps fueled his control over the kenku.

%% u4329:0-52 %%
Delwath: Dan, do you still think we can pull it through this?

%% u4330:0-52 %%
Wellby: Uh, it makes it seem like a worse and worse mistake.

%% u4331:0-8 %%
DM: To what?

%% u4332:0-45 %%
Wellby: Give a scroll of Connect Abyss to this plane.

%% u4333:0-16 %%
DM: Not necessarily.

%% u4334:0-49, u4335:0-12 %%
Wellby: I guess that's kind of what I meant. That's fair. That's fair.

%% u4336:0-24 %%
DM: I mean, it's not better.

%% u4337:0-8 %%
Wellby: [laughs]

%% u4338:0-19 %%
DM: But it's different.

%% u4339:0-13, u4340:0-111 %%
Delwath: It's, it's... I still think that if Fausto was working for Apollyon, Apollyon doesn't want to destroy Taelgar by crashing it.

%% u4341:0-51 %%
DM: Yeah, remember Cha'mutte and Apollyon were enemies.

%% u4342:0-6 %%
Delwath: Right.

%% u4343:0-5 %%
Wellby: True.

%% u4344:0-3, u4345:0-25, u4346:0-86 %%
Delwath: Um, this demon I think was... He w- the demon was fighting with Cha'mutte before, I believe, Cha'mutte was defeated.

%% u4347:0-28 %%
DM: But to the north, you think?

%% u4348:0-14, u4349:0-25, u4350:0-43 %%
Delwath: North of here, near our home, I believe. So I just saw a vision that Yezali sent me.

%% u4351:0-7, u4352:0-17, u4353:0-16, u4354:0-29 %%
DM: Perhaps the first to fall early in the war when the demons issued forth.

%% u4355:0-18, u4356:0-56 %%
Delwath: I suspect as much. Have you met other elves who have come out in the world?

%% u4357:0-4 %%
DM: Few.

%% u4358:0-13 %%
Delwath: It's so rare.

%% u4359:0-25, u4360:0-8, u4361:0-14 %%
DM: There are not many of us. There is one false elf.

%% u4362:0-17, u4363:0-22 %%
Delwath: The green dragon? You met him, I gather.

%% u4364:0-27, u4365:0-12, u4366:0-23, u4367:0-12, u4368:0-35 %%
DM: I stayed with his tribe for a few weeks. He tried to enchant me. He tried to, he tried to put a geas, geas on me.

%% u4369:0-11 %%
Kenzo: Put a what?

%% u4370:0-1, u4371:0-46 %%
DM: A charm, a, a command to perform a task for him.

%% u4372:0-18 %%
Delwath: What was the task?

%% u4373:0-6, u4374:0-20, u4375:0-15, u4376:0-14, u4377:0-13, u4378:0-16, u4379:0-32, u4380:0-14, u4381:0-17, u4382:0-33, u4383:0-32, u4384:0-20, u4385:0-30 %%
DM: It was come to this village and bring Jordo out to justice for refusing, for refusing to, for refusing to accept his rule. And of course, his magic failed. I believe he knew that elves were difficult to sway in such a way, but he tried anyway. You know, that moment I ran...

%% u4386:0-13 %%
Delwath: Did you know-

%% u4387:0-14 %%
DM: And he changed.

%% u4388:0-43 %%
Delwath: ... did you, he changed to a dragon, right?

%% u4389:0-45, u4390:0-45, u4391:0-39 %%
DM: Fortunately, I had fought dragons in the war. I was not wholly unprepared for such a thing. But he was far beyond my skill to face.

%% u4392:0-4, u4393:0-77, u4394:0-15 %%
Delwath: Hmm. I never had the misfortune of fighting a dragon until yes, two, two days ago? Three days ago?

%% u4395:0-26 %%
DM: Like three days ago, yeah.

%% u4396:0-15, u4397:0-7, u4398:0-28, u4399:0-37 %%
Delwath: Three days ago. We, uh, did not expect him to change and were, I think, wholly unprepared.

%% u4400:0-23, u4401:0-31, u4402:0-38, u4403:0-34, u4404:0-16, u4405:0-40, u4406:0-30, u4407:0-7, u4408:0-25 %%
DM: But I found this place, and I've been safe here now for five years now. S- no, more than that. I lose track of these human years. Almost 10 years. I was able to meet Jordo before he died. He was an old man when I came. He was, he was an impressive man.

%% u4409:0-49 %%
Delwath: Do you know anything about where he got his jade?

%% u4410:0-8 %%
Unknown: [laughs]

%% u4411:0-83, u4412:0-40 %%
DM: He found it in the forest embedded in a fallen god tree, they call them, one of the trees that were the homes of our people.

%% u4413:0-23 %%
Delwath: Do you know what it is?

%% u4414:0-3, u4415:0-46 %%
DM: No. It has something to do with dreams, I believe.

%% u4416:0-56 %%
Delwath: My friend Kenzo has another piece of the, the same jade.

%% u4417:0-14 %%
DM: The same jade?

%% u4418:0-7 %%
Delwath: Indeed.

%% u4419:0-23 %%
DM: Do you know what it is?

%% u4420:0-25 %%
Kenzo: Uh, it's in my soul item.

%% u4421:0-3, u4422:0-6, u4423:0-121, u4424:0-53, u4425:0-11, u4426:0-72, u4427:0-47 %%
DM: Um, he is, uh, although there's a tinge of sadness to everything he does, he is very happy to be speaking to another elf, and he is, his curiosity is piqued a little bit about this jade. He seems... I mean, elves are always somewhat tricky to read, but there's nothing... You know what? He seems pretty open and honest.

%% u4428:0-65 %%
Wellby: He didn't, for example, get the geas, "Come here and kill Jordo."

%% u4429:0-32, u4430:0-18, u4431:0-17, u4432:0-47 %%
DM: Well, Jordo died five years ago, so even if he was- Hmm. He could, um ... I mean, it lasts for, like, 30 days, right?

%% u4433:0-53 %%
Delwath: Spend a feat and make sure he's not under any spells.

%% u4434:0-10 %%
DM: You could.

%% u4435:0-12 %%
Kenzo: Any of that?

%% u4436:0-5 %%
DM: Yeah.

%% u4437:0-5 %%
Kenzo: Yeah.

%% u4438:0-49, u4439:0-32, u4440:0-48 %%
DM: He's not under any spells. He's not super strong. He's, like, level three or four. He's not, he's not as powerful as you, for sure.

%% u4441:0-76, u4442:0-41 %%
Kenzo: Well, he's g- we're gonna need him in the battle against the, uh, dragon, so be good to have a sense of his abilities.

%% u4443:0-13 %%
DM: Yeah, indeed.

%% u4444:0-81 %%
Delwath: I think the important thing is just that he's not currently under any compulsion.

%% u4445:0-11 %%
Kenzo: Yeah. Yeah.

%% u4446:0-20 %%
DM: Right, right, right.

%% u4447:0-29 %%
Kenzo: Uh, what did he ask me again?

%% u4448:0-32, u4449:0-17 %%
DM: What, what is the piece of jade? What is the jade?

%% u4450:0-37 %%
Kenzo: What do you know of the Sorcerer Rai?

%% u4451:0-47 %%
DM: I know he killed Cha'mutte with his companions.

%% u4452:0-5 %%
Kenzo: Yeah.

%% u4453:0-33, u4454:0-27 %%
DM: Fought in many wars to the south, not where we were fighting.

%% u4455:0-37, u4456:0-39, u4457:0-36 %%
Kenzo: You've lived here for five years now. What's the relationship that the, uh... What's the name of this tribe again?

%% u4458:0-8 %%
DM: Te'kula.

%% u4459:0-62 %%
Kenzo: What's the relationship with the Te'kula and the Sorcerer Rai?

%% u4460:0-28, u4461:0-61, u4462:0-2, u4463:0-84, u4464:0-18, u4465:0-44, u4466:0-25, u4467:0-68, u4468:0-27, u4469:0-23 %%
DM: Alayah speaks of him as a... [sighs] There's not a good word in your tongue to explain it. A, a, a friend, but a friend from a, from an older generation, a, a wiser friend, a, a, a friend who is... A, a friend who is an elder, who is a, um... There's no word. I, uh...

See, there's a word in Elvish that basically means, like, one of the originals, like, one of the very oldest elves from-

%% u4470:0-5 %%
Kenzo: Right.

%% u4471:0-81, u4472:0-7 %%
DM: ... from, like, the elves who've been around the- A, a friend who aspires to such heights.

%% u4473:0-4, u4474:0-78 %%
Kenzo: Hmm. And what of the, uh, what's the name of the, um, the, the god they're, um, uh-

%% u4475:0-7 %%
DM: Aasimti.

%% u4476:0-12, u4477:0-25 %%
Kenzo: ... Aasimti. And, and what of Aasimti?

%% u4478:0-36, u4479:0-14, u4480:0-31, u4481:0-64, u4482:0-37, u4483:0-22, u4484:0-44, u4485:0-32, u4486:0-29 %%
DM: There are many spirits they worship. Aasimti seems, in their prayers, stronger than the others. The, the, the spirits, the gods of these people have little power to influence this world. They, they make signs, sometimes grant p- prayers to those who are, uh, attuned to the divine realm. But Aasimti seems to do more.

%% u4487:0-4 %%
Kenzo: Hmm.

%% u4488:0-34, u4489:0-35, u4490:0-14, u4491:0-26, u4492:0-3, u4493:0-12, u4494:0-32, u4495:0-52 %%
DM: Unusual from what I know of these, of their religion, what I've heard. Even they seem in awe of Aasimti's power. Po- There are... Common has so few words for the, that get at the mea- heart of the meaning of things.

%% u4496:0-20 %%
Kenzo: Such crude language.

%% u4497:0-16 %%
Delwath: Not like Elvish.

%% u4498:0-16 %%
DM: Not like Elvish.

%% u4499:0-20 %%
Kenzo: Crude human tongues.

%% u4500:0-8, u4501:0-76, u4502:0-63, u4503:0-50, u4504:0-34, u4505:0-27, u4506:0-24, u4507:0-26, u4508:0-16, u4509:0-16, u4510:0-82, u4511:0-12, u4512:0-4, u4513:0-7, u4514:0-30 %%
DM: It is... It is a, a, a, a, an awe combi- combined with surprise, but also of delight. Not, not in the sort of laughing, happy delight way, but in the feeling blessed we are gifted with this power way. That is how they speak of Aasimti.

I believe somehow he has... They, I should say they. They always refer to them, Tanshi, by they. They have gotten more power somehow since the jade has been here, and they are the ones who shifted this village here to this pocket realm, this safe haven.

%% u4515:0-39 %%
Kenzo: Is, is there a way to speak to Aasimti?

%% u4516:0-13, u4517:0-41 %%
DM: You can pray. I don't know if they would listen to you.

%% u4518:0-3, u4519:0-13, u4520:0-25, u4521:0-10, u4522:0-51, u4523:0-7, u4524:0-71 %%
Delwath: Um, he's resting. Do you know if any of the elders are out in the world or in a place that could be found? I would greatly wish to speak to somebody who was alive at the fall of Drankor.

%% u4525:0-4, u4526:0-50, u4527:0-12, u4528:0-19, u4529:0-54, u4530:0-19, u4531:0-46, u4532:0-35, u4533:0-49, u4534:0-25, u4535:0-44 %%
DM: Hmm. They are even harder to find than they used to be. Few of us...

You have been gone. Many of our, almost all of our generation went to war. You know we were... We had no doubt we were doing the right thing, even though many of the elders were uncertain if we should risk it all on this fight. You must remember we were so certain of the righteousness of our task.

%% u4536:0-4 %%
Delwath: Yes.

%% u4537:0-21, u4538:0-16, u4539:0-27, u4540:0-14, u4541:0-45, u4542:0-27, u4543:0-9, u4544:0-10, u4545:0-26, u4546:0-13, u4547:0-13, u4548:0-30, u4549:0-19, u4550:0-18, u4551:0-18, u4552:0-38, u4553:0-10, u4554:0-29, u4555:0-69, u4556:0-55 %%
DM: But so few came back. We have changed. Everybody is in hiding now. Nobody speaks. There are no councils in the Feywild anymore.

That is part of why I left. I, I hope someday to find more of us and maybe, I don't know, maybe change. Maybe the 38th generation will look outward again. It is almost time.

But I do not know. I, this is what I've been looking for. I've been, I've been hiding here because as I fled, Grimbaskal promised to kill me if I, he ever saw me again. So I have not left this place until, well, he is alive.

%% u4557:0-26 %%
Seeker: We're on his bad side too.

%% u4558:0-57 %%
Delwath: He has sent the Deno- He has sent the what's-their-tribe?

%% u4559:0-13, u4560:0-15 %%
DM: Deno'qai. The Bezek- Be'zeni.

%% u4561:0-29, u4562:0-52, u4563:0-22 %%
Delwath: He sent the Be'zeni after us. I do not believe he meant to reveal his dragon form. He killed six of them.

%% u4564:0-26, u4565:0-51 %%
DM: He is very careful. He has woven conflict in this forest in a very subtle way.

%% u4566:0-19 %%
Delwath: I think he was, um,

%% u4567:0-20, u4568:0-35 %%
Kenzo: He was not expecting an attack, and he was not expecting—

%% u4569:0-18 %%
Delwath: Such near success.

%% u4570:0-40, u4571:0-23, u4572:0-87, u4573:0-33, u4574:0-18 %%
DM: I think he panicked and became a dragon. Where he could be seen? He did not turn into his dragon form and chase me until I was well outside the village. Having left, having fled after he tried to charm me.

%% u4575:0-12 %%
Delwath: He murdered-

%% u4576:0-44 %%
DM: He let me go and then tried to hunt me later.

%% u4577:0-35, u4578:0-22 %%
Delwath: ... who murdered all the witnesses. Although one is, um...

%% u4579:0-16 %%
Kenzo: The body of one.

%% u4580:0-21, u4581:0-24, u4582:0-76 %%
Delwath: Yeah, we, we may, we, we brought one. [laughs] I won't, no, I don't want to say that. We didn't bring a dead one, you know.

%% u4583:0-7 %%
Wellby: 'Cause-

%% u4584:0-18 %%
Delwath: Um, I think, uh...

%% u4585:0-13, u4586:0-16 %%
DM: I think, yes, we are hoping to—

%% u4587:0-34 %%
Delwath: Address the problem of Grimbaskal.

%% u4588:0-61 %%
Kenzo: The histories say that there was a conclave of these dragons.

%% u4589:0-23 %%
DM: I fought that conclave.

%% u4590:0-18 %%
Delwath: Is he one of them?

%% u4591:0-3 %%
DM: No.

%% u4592:0-9 %%
Delwath: He's not?

%% u4593:0-4 %%
Kenzo: Hmm.

%% u4594:0-18 %%
Delwath: Do you know which-

%% u4595:0-40, u4596:0-98 %%
DM: They were all killed, five dragons dead. To the east of Chardon, there is a giant scar along the river where nothing grows where they fell.

%% u4597:0-35 %%
Kenzo: We passed it. Ah, we did pass that.

%% u4598:0-13, u4599:0-37 %%
Delwath: We passed it. Do you know what he's doing out east?

%% u4600:0-12, u4601:0-11 %%
DM: This dragon, Grimbaskal?

%% u4602:0-81 %%
Delwath: He goes, he goes east with men and women from the village, and they never return.

%% u4603:0-17 %%
DM: That is his lair.

%% u4604:0-6 %%
Delwath: Where?

%% u4605:0-12, u4606:0-14, u4607:0-40 %%
DM: To the east, in the forest. I know not what he does with the people.

%% u4608:0-23, u4609:0-23 %%
Delwath: The, the forest is, uh, unhappy about the lair.

%% u4610:0-23 %%
DM: I know. I have felt it.

%% u4611:0-29 %%
Seeker: What do you think he's after?

%% u4612:0-43, u4613:0-38 %%
DM: I think he wants everybody in the forest to bow down to him and be in fear of him.

%% u4614:0-12, u4615:0-19 %%
Seeker: Is this, um, typical of dragons?

%% u4616:0-64, u4617:0-19, u4618:0-22, u4619:0-72, u4620:0-56, u4621:0-30 %%
DM: I would not say there is anything that is typical about dragons. There is nothing... Each dragon is unique. I do not believe his ambitions stretch beyond this forest, but I believe over a generation or two, he will turn this place into a den of paranoid collaborators.

%% u4622:0-11 %%
Delwath: And ghosts.

%% u4623:0-29, u4624:0-16 %%
DM: And ghosts, indeed, those who refuse to serve.

%% u4625:0-43 %%
Seeker: The change, the change is already underway.

%% u4626:0-25 %%
Kenzo: What do you mean, Seeker?

%% u4627:0-33, u4628:0-5, u4629:0-2, u4630:0-14 %%
DM: He's had almost 50 years to work. More, 20 years to work.

%% u4631:0-53 %%
Seeker: Well, Netar, for example, is a paranoid collaborator.

%% u4632:0-4 %%
Kenzo: Hmm.

%% u4633:0-4 %%
DM: Yes.

%% u4634:0-20 %%
Delwath: And Zevi is a ghost.

%% u4635:0-5 %%
DM: Zevi.

%% u4636:0-12 %%
Kenzo: Zevi's what?

%% u4637:0-8 %%
Delwath: A ghost.

%% u4638:0-5 %%
DM: Yeah.

%% u4639:0-8 %%
Kenzo: A ghost?

%% u4640:0-13 %%
Delwath: Well, I mean-

%% u4641:0-14 %%
Kenzo: Oh, yeah, yeah.

%% u4642:0-23 %%
Delwath: ... but, but he's dead.

%% u4643:0-40 %%
Kenzo: Are there, are there other dragons here?

%% u4644:0-14 %%
DM: In the forest?

%% u4645:0-4 %%
Kenzo: Yes.

%% u4646:0-57, u4647:0-18, u4648:0-26, u4649:0-26, u4650:0-21, u4651:0-13, u4652:0-52, u4653:0-24, u4654:0-21 %%
DM: Not that I know of. It is rare for them to work together. The conclave was a unusual event in our lore. We spoke much in the camps in these very forests 200 years ago of the lore of dragons and why they banded together. But I believe those were Cha'mutte's generals.

%% u4655:0-59 %%
Kenzo: So you do not sense that this, uh, that Griswald, Griswald?

%% u4656:0-11 %%
DM: Grimbaskal.

%% u4657:0-11 %%
Kenzo: Grimbaskal.

%% u4658:0-3 %%
DM: Cl-

%% u4659:0-101 %%
Kenzo: That, that Grimbaskal is, is connected to that generation of dragons, the con- the original conclave?

%% u4660:0-16 %%
DM: He is too young.

%% u4661:0-4 %%
Kenzo: Hmm.

%% u4662:0-42, u4663:0-25 %%
DM: I believe he's no more than 200 years old. Still young for a dragon.

%% u4664:0-4 %%
Kenzo: Hmm.

%% u4665:0-37, u4666:0-29, u4667:0-40, u4668:0-60, u4669:0-46 %%
DM: He's not one of those ancient terrors that can shatter cities, yet. I believe he aspires to be such a thing. And with a f- another 100 years turning this forest into his empire, I believe he could achieve that power.

%% u4670:0-4 %%
Kenzo: Hmm.

%% u4671:0-36, u4672:0-24 %%
DM: But the dragons of the conclave were far stronger than he is.

%% u4673:0-53, u4674:0-41, u4675:0-40 %%
Seeker: We're interested in him not just because we recently, uh, tangled with him, but because we are, uh, concerned that we may have to again.

%% u4676:0-35 %%
Delwath: Have to again tangle with him, yes.

%% u4677:0-32, u4678:0-9, u4679:0-16, u4680:0-41 %%
DM: If you did indeed challenge him, if he was disturbed enough to shift his form where he could be seen-

%% u4681:0-9 %%
Seeker: He indeed.

%% u4682:0-3, u4683:0-35, u4684:0-46, u4685:0-18 %%
DM: ... he will have retreated to his lair. Oh. Uh, I don't want to fight him in his lair. That sounds scary.

%% u4686:0-31 %%
Kenzo: Is there a way to draw him out?

%% u4687:0-4, u4688:0-23 %%
DM: Wait till he feels stronger.

%% u4689:0-41 %%
Seeker: Per- perhaps we can simply be on our way.

%% u4690:0-24 %%
DM: But if he's retreated...

%% u4691:0-49 %%
Delwath: There's a chance that we could go loot his house.

%% u4692:0-51 %%
Seeker: I was thinking the same but didn't say it out loud.

%% u4693:0-5 %%
Delwath: Um...

%% u4694:0-76 %%
DM: Wait, why would you k- why would you want to loot his just to get the stuff?

%% u4695:0-67 %%
Delwath: Yeah, and, but also w- as part of maybe, like, getting the village.

%% u4696:0-6 %%
DM: Right.

%% u4697:0-102 %%
Delwath: Like, if he's not in the village, there's more chance that there's some faction that might believe us.

%% u4698:0-26 %%
DM: Right, we could turn them.

%% u4699:0-3, u4700:0-11, u4701:0-44, u4702:0-3, u4703:0-2, u4704:0-13, u4705:0-12, u4706:0-55, u4707:0-32 %%
Delwath: Um, it has been a bittersweet pleasure to catch up with you. Uh, I, I... Delwath, I think, um, the news you bring is weighing heavy on me and I may go take a short walk in the forest.

%% u4708:0-13 %%
DM: I understand.

%% u4709:0-53 %%
Seeker: I have some questions if you have a few minutes more.

%% u4710:0-36 %%
DM: I will speak with you, friend dwarf.

%% u4711:0-24, u4712:0-25, u4713:0-25 %%
Seeker: Thank you. You mentioned searching the Feywild and gathering in the Feywild.

%% u4714:0-35, u4715:0-8, u4716:0-11 %%
DM: Find your way again in the Feywild. That's a good point.

%% u4717:0-79, u4718:0-127, u4719:0-24, u4720:0-12 %%
Seeker: I'm a student of the lore of the Feywild, but I've not, uh, yet visited myself. Our party has wondered if in our travels we might be able to pass through there and in that way travel rapidly across distance on this plane. Is that a possibility?

%% u4721:0-114 %%
DM: The stream of time that runs in the Feywild is not always in sync with the stream of time that runs in this world.

%% u4722:0-7 %%
Seeker: Mm-hmm.

%% u4723:0-30, u4724:0-77, u4725:0-13, u4726:0-72 %%
DM: If you know how to control it, you can travel for weeks in the Feywild and emerge only hours after you left. If you don't, you can travel for weeks in the Feywild and emerge years after you left.

%% u4727:0-27 %%
Wellby: Can such a skill be taught?

%% u4728:0-12 %%
Delwath: What's that?

%% u4729:0-27 %%
Wellby: Can such a skill be taught?

%% u4730:0-4, u4731:0-96 %%
Delwath: Hmm. Wait here, Del. I have some quest- I have a few things Delwath wants to do, but finish this bit.

%% u4732:0-2, u4733:0-18, u4734:0-4, u4735:0-35, u4736:0-10, u4737:0-10, u4738:0-15 %%
DM: He sort of... I mean, "Yes. The elves teach such skill to their guides and wanderers. It is not easy."

%% u4739:0-40, u4740:0-14 %%
Seeker: I'd imagine such guides are hard to find in these days.

%% u4741:0-21, u4742:0-7, u4743:0-14 %%
DM: In these days indeed. Before, it was easier.

%% u4744:0-7 %%
Seeker: Mm-hmm.

%% u4745:0-46, u4746:0-29, u4747:0-28 %%
DM: I've traveled to the Feywild several times and it is, it is a special place, but it's hard to get to now.

%% u4748:0-6 %%
Seeker: It is?

%% u4749:0-15, u4750:0-28, u4751:0-10, u4752:0-34, u4753:0-62, u4754:0-76 %%
DM: More dangerous. Many of the, many of the old trees, the God trees, the Deno'qai call them, many of our cities indeed once had connections to the Feywild. Controlled and, and modulated by the elven magic, they were safe to transit.

%% u4755:0-4 %%
Seeker: Hmm.

%% u4756:0-25, u4757:0-20, u4758:0-44, u4759:0-35 %%
DM: Some portals still remain in near these trees. With the cities gone, but they are no longer controlled, protected by the elves.

%% u4760:0-7 %%
Seeker: Mm-hmm.

%% u4761:0-37, u4762:0-64, u4763:0-18, u4764:0-41 %%
DM: So stepping through is always a risk. You do not know what might be waiting for you on the other side. Not all Fey are... Not all Fey are charming and mischievous.

%% u4765:0-13 %%
Wellby: That we know.

%% u4766:0-36 %%
Seeker: Yes, some are charming and terrible.

%% u4767:0-43 %%
DM: [laughs] Some are mischievous and terrible.

%% u4768:0-15 %%
Wellby: Is Agata a Fey?

%% u4769:0-5 %%
DM: Yeah.

%% u4770:0-25 %%
Wellby: Do you know how to guide?

%% u4771:0-25 %%
DM: I do not have such skill.

%% u4772:0-27 %%
Wellby: Does anyone in the village?

%% u4773:0-65, u4774:0-35, u4775:0-4 %%
DM: No, I don't believe any of these people have been to the Feywild. They think this is the dream world. Not-

%% u4776:0-22 %%
Wellby: Is it the dream world?

%% u4777:0-20, u4778:0-8, u4779:0-25, u4780:0-45, u4781:0-34, u4782:0-10, u4783:0-25 %%
DM: The dream world is a place of illusion, a, a deep echo, a place even more strange and impermeable and unsettled than the Feywild itself. This is a, a demiplane of some kind.

%% u4784:0-17 %%
Seeker: Where we are now?

%% u4785:0-17 %%
DM: Where we are now.

%% u4786:0-20 %%
Seeker: What is a demiplane?

%% u4787:0-79, u4788:0-30, u4789:0-56 %%
DM: Well, strong magic can create pockets in the fabric of reality, spaces that are outside of our material world. You may be familiar with such things as bags of holding.

%% u4790:0-18 %%
Seeker: Or portable holes.

%% u4791:0-23 %%
DM: Indeed, portable holes.

%% u4792:0-67, u4793:0-31 %%
Delwath: Wasn't the plane where we were hiding from Shakun, hiding in the... Was that a demiplane or was it-

%% u4794:0-24 %%
DM: That was Shakun's realm.

%% u4795:0-18 %%
Delwath: That was Shakun's.

%% u4796:0-22 %%
Seeker: That was a real plane.

%% u4797:0-75 %%
DM: That was one of the outer planes, one of the old spirit realms of the gods.

%% u4798:0-31 %%
Seeker: 'Cause it was stable and large.

%% u4799:0-8 %%
DM: Exactly.

%% u4800:0-61 %%
Seeker: But this is, this one is short-lived and small that we're in.

%% u4801:0-50, u4802:0-47 %%
DM: This where we are, although large for a demiplane, only extends about 20 miles in every direction.

%% u4803:0-9, u4804:0-20 %%
Seeker: What, uh, what's at the edges?

%% u4805:0-55 %%
DM: Well, you must have come through a twisted forest path.

%% u4806:0-7 %%
Seeker: We did.

%% u4807:0-17, u4808:0-23 %%
DM: You saw echoes of hopes or fears perhaps.

%% u4809:0-7 %%
Seeker: Indeed.

%% u4810:0-52, u4811:0-59 %%
DM: That is the edge. I think it is maintained somehow by this god, Aasimti, and somehow through the jade as a focus.

%% u4812:0-20, u4813:0-23, u4814:0-63 %%
Seeker: The jade as a focus? Does that mean removing that piece of jade from inside this plane would destabilize it?

%% u4815:0-26, u4816:0-81 %%
DM: I believe it would return. The boundaries would fade, and we would be back in the material world of Taelgar.

%% u4817:0-59 %%
Wellby: Which would be a problem if there was still a dragon there.

%% u4818:0-86 %%
DM: That would be a problem for this village and for me if there was still a dragon there.

%% u4819:0-36, u4820:0-73, u4821:0-18 %%
Wellby: Real quick, um, just among us, do... Is it that we want to collect these pieces of jade and put them together? Is that a goal or?

%% u4822:0-118, u4823:0-112, u4824:0-11 %%
Kenzo: I think meta game, or just not even meta game, but I think, I think that, I think if we're able to collect the pieces, one, we'll learn more, and two, there's a chance that there may be a restorative, res- a restoring power on the, the prison.

%% u4825:0-22 %%
Wellby: Oh, interesting. Yeah.

%% u4826:0-48, u4827:0-14, u4828:0-25 %%
Kenzo: That the breaking of the jade was related to the breaking of... How do you say Apollonia?

%% u4829:0-9 %%
DM: Apollyon.

%% u4830:0-9 %%
Wellby: Apollyon.

%% u4831:0-10 %%
Kenzo: Apa- what?

%% u4832:0-9 %%
DM: Apollyon.

%% u4833:0-21, u4834:0-11 %%
Kenzo: Related to Apollyon's liberation.

%% u4835:0-3 %%
DM: Um.

%% u4836:0-11 %%
Kenzo: Delwath is-

%% u4837:0-28 %%
DM: What did Delwath want to do?

%% u4838:0-38, u4839:0-30 %%
Delwath: He's taking a long walk in the forest. He's looking for more berries.

%% u4840:0-7 %%
DM: Mm-hmm.

%% u4841:0-69 %%
Delwath: Or other things that he could cook that would be reminiscent of home.

%% u4842:0-7 %%
DM: Mm-hmm.

%% u4843:0-12, u4844:0-27 %%
Delwath: Then he's... Is there any running water?

%% u4845:0-86 %%
DM: Uh, yeah. Some, like, I don't know, a quarter mile from the village, there's a stream.

%% u4846:0-56 %%
Delwath: Um, he's gonna spend a while trying to talk to Aaviskar.

%% u4847:0-5 %%

There's heat and intense waves of hatred and anger from the shadow. It's not the flat, emotionless landscape of the Shadowfell, but somewhere worse, the Abyss perhaps. But still you reach and you twist with your hand, s- figuring if you can at least find the anchor that holds this portal open, and turn it just right, the connection will sever no matter where it leads.

And as you stretch deeper, you find what you are looking for, and you grab with your hand and start to twist, but suddenly something feels wrong. The strands of this other plane are deeply interwoven and entwined with the fabric of reality itself. This is no mere portal as you suspected, as you assumed, and your hand is caught.

It's caught in the interstitial fabric of the reality of the material plane itself. Some thread of the Abyss or whatever dark place has merged with the land here, holding you as you try thinking this was a simple portal to close. And you think, "No wonder the corruption has spread so far from here." And too late you try to retreat, try to pull yourself away.

Delwath: I was trapped in that realm as well, until they found a way to release me, or something released me, and I found a way to commune with them. I saw a vision of Yezali's past when he, when they spent all their energy defending a village north of here during the Great War.

%% u6939:0-13, u6940:0-2, u6941:0-11, u6942:0-32, u6943:0-13, u6944:0-69, u6945:0-22 %%

%% u6946:0-4 %%

%% u6947:0-32 %%

%% u6948:0-5, u6949:0-22, u6950:0-34 %%

%% u6952:0-7, u6953:0-33, u6954:0-11, u6955:0-25, u6956:0-43, u6957:0-34, u6958:0-27 %%
Delwath: Perhaps you could offer a prayer to them. I think the connection of one who has heard stories of Yezali as they were before being trapped in this other place, I think it would help them.

%% u6959:0-8, u6960:0-15, u6961:0-30, u6962:0-20, u6963:0-21, u6964:0-34, u6965:0-46, u6966:0-57 %%
DM: "It was, it was long ago when I was just a little girl. My grandmother would mourn the lost still, tradition she got from her mother. Not just the lost people, the lost Tanshi too. I think there were more, three or four she talked about."

%% u6967:0-28 %%

%% u6968:0-6, u6969:0-2, u6970:0-28, u6971:0-10, u6972:0-29, u6973:0-9, u6974:0-43, u6975:0-10, u6976:0-16, u6977:0-96, u6978:0-20, u6979:0-43, u6980:0-27, u6981:0-50, u6982:0-21, u6983:0-30 %%
DM: "I do. I, I would not neglect my duty.

There was, uh... There was, uh, Yalik'i, spirit of crisp, cold weather, of frost in the trees.

There was, there was Midri, the spirit of the dirt and the soil and the nutrients that flow from tree to tree in the forest.

There was K'onisati, spirit of butterflies and colorful insects. Everything that transformed from one to another form, caterpillars especially.

And there was Yezali, spirit of—the nighttime hunt."

%% u6984:0-3 %%

%% u6985:0-36 %%

%% u6986:0-6, u6987:0-11 %%

%% u6996:0-4 %%

DM: Yeah, right. That's true. [laughs] Um, from the side of the head.

And, and you continue to travel through this surreal landscape. A- and it seems like it should be tough going, but a- as you, as you travel, um, it almost seems like the vegetation is, it's not getting out of your way to make things easier. It's getting out of your way to make you visible. Like you might be, you'd be trying to sort of like sneak around a tree. You'd be trying to stay in the undergrowth. The undergrowth would sort of move around you so that you're- Mm ... opening up space, and the snakes are watching.

When you get by mid-afternoon, uh, you can see, um, you can see in front of you maybe a half mile off, there's like a clear, a clear sort of glimpse of the trees occasionally. Half mile is probably too far, like 4- 400, like 300 yards, something like that. You'll occasionally see a glimpse of the tree, of, um, a much bigger tree, but something that looks very different from the god trees that you were seeing before.

This is a massively thick like banyan tree with like 20 trunks grown together. It must be, uh, it must be like 40 feet across, but it's not tall. It's maybe no more than 40, 50 feet tall. It's got this massive canopy, and there's vines hanging down from it everywhere.

Um, there's, the ground starts to get spongy and difficult to walk on, like it's almost getting damp, moist. Um, and the, uh, um, and the, uh, there's a whiff of rot in the air. [coughs] And every now and then, um, a snake will look at you. [coughs]

And as you begin to get closer to this tree, one snake turns, and its mouth face grows enormous, turning into a dragon's maw. And it says, "Have you come to apologize and seek my favor? Repent the error of your ways? Bow before me like all others who dwell in my territory? Or have you come to die?"
